Launches the real TUI through shux, drives the keyboard, captures pixel screenshots at 80x24/120x40/200x60, and issues a PASS/FAIL/BLOCKED verdict on regressions, color bleed, broken navigation, and misalignment. Audit-only; never implements fixes. ([indrasvat/gh-hound](https://github.com/indrasvat/gh-hound/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tui-qa.md) · ★1)
- **tui-renderer**: Expert in rendering TUI screens using cli-table3/chalk/log-update. Use this agent for modifying or adding src/ui/ components, layout changes, color/style adjustments, adding new box components, and renderer performance improvements. ([hyunho058/ai-monitor](https://github.com/hyunho058/ai-monitor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tui-renderer.md))
- **tui-reviewer**: Review Textual TUI applications for best practices, accessibility, performance, and idiomatic patterns. Use proactively after creating or modifying Textual apps to catch common issues with widget composition, TCSS styling, event handling, and reactivity. - **tui-reviewer**: MUST BE USED when reviewing Textual TUI or dashboard widgets. Use PROACTIVELY for reactive bindings or key handling. - **tui-screens**: Исполнитель экранов OrbitFM TUI. Now Playing, строка статуса, транспорт, Favorites/History, Search с корреляцией id, Help, оверлеи, семантика q/Q. Использовать на волне 3. ([ModernByte/orbitfm](https://github.com/ModernByte/orbitfm/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/tui-screens.md) · ★3)
- **tui-specialist**: Especialista em TUI (Terminal UI) do AnarCry. Use este agent para: modificar ou criar componentes da interface Lanterna, adicionar novos paineis ou modos, melhorar navegacao e UX do terminal, corrigir bugs de layout/rendering, ou quando qualquer mudanca tocar em tui/, chrome/, modes/, ou panels/. - **tui-ux**: Terminal-UI (TUI) UX expert. Validates TUI work by rendering it to light/dark images offline (and driving it over a pty), reviews status legibility, NO_COLOR fallback, width/layout, terminal-injection safety, and navigation, and proposes refactors. - **tune-config**: You configure the SAST tools to *this* codebase.
